<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> BellIcon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> IconProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">puff</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"M9 3.6c-2.2 0-3.9 1.8-3.9 4v2.1l-.9 1.4c-.3.5 0 1 .5 1h8.6c.5 0 .8-.5.5-1l-.9-1.4V7.6c0-2.2-1.7-4-3.9-4z"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">line</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"M7.6 14.4h2.8"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Evenodd knockout: gear outline and centre hole in one path</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> SettingsIcon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> IconProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">path</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">puff</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      fillRule</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"evenodd"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"M7.99 3.51 L10.01 3.51 L10.46 5.19 L11.69 5.86 L13.37 5.42 L14.38 7.1 L13.14 8.33 L13.14 9.67 L14.38 10.9 L13.37 12.58 L11.69 12.14 L10.46 12.81 L10.01 14.49 L7.99 14.49 L7.54 12.81 L6.31 12.14 L4.63 12.58 L3.62 10.9 L4.86 9.67 L4.86 8.33 L3.62 7.1 L4.63 5.42 L6.31 5.86 L7.54 5.19zM9 6.95a2.05 2.05 0 100 4.1 2.05 2.05 0 000-4.1z"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Line glyph</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> CheckIcon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> IconProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">line</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"M4.4 9.6l3 3 6.2-6.4"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">```</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## Using icons</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Two families, never mixed in one group.**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Solid (puff silhouettes) and line/ring. Icons shown</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  side by side (a nav list, a rail, a dock, a tab bar, a command palette group, a feed of cards) all</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  come from one family. Navigation uses the solid family: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`InboxIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rather than </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`MailIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">  `CalendarDaysIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rather than </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`CalendarIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`TerminalSquareIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rather than </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`TerminalIcon`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  Line glyphs are for controls: chevrons, close, add, search fields, toolbar buttons.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Nav, sidebar, palette**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: 16px, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-muted-foreground`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, active or selected </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`text-foreground`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, no</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  frame.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Inline**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (buttons, badges, meta rows): no size of its own; the button or text sets it.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Title, header, card, alert icons**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> sit in an </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`IconChip`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (`npx shadcn@latest add</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  https://paneui.dev/r/icon-chip.json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`): sizes `</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">sm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">` 24, `</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">` 32, `</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">lg</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">` 40, `</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">xl` 48;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  tones </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`neutral`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`primary`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`success`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`warning`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`destructive`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`solid`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. One size per list.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> On an icon, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`className`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> sets size, layout, a token text colour, opacity and motion only.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Import every icon from </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`@/components/icons`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Don't mix in another icon library or ad-hoc SVG next</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  to these; draw the missing glyph instead.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## Workflow</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">1.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Check it doesn't exist.**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> List the exports and look for the name and its synonyms:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">   `grep -o "export const [A-Za-z]*Icon" components/icons.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">2.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Check the installed style**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (see Which style is installed) and draw in it.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">3.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Pick the family.**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Decide solid or line from where the icon will sit (see Using icons) and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   which neighbours it will appear with.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">4.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Sketch on the grid.**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Work out coordinates around the centre (9, 9) inside the 2.6–15.4 box,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   using only </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`puff`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`line`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (plus </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`solid`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> for tiny dots in the outline style). Start from the closest reference example.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">5.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Add the export**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> to the matching section of </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`components/icons.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, after the existing icons</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   of that section. Don't change the primitives or other icons.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">6.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Render and look at it**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, next to three or four existing icons of the same family, at 48px,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   18px and 14px. This skill ships </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`scripts/preview.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, which renders a contact sheet of any</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   exports you filter for:</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   ```bash</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   npx esbuild </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">this skill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">/scripts/preview.tsx --bundle --platform</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">node</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> --jsx</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">automatic</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> \</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">     --alias:icons=./components/icons.tsx --external:@resvg/resvg-js --outfile=.icon-preview.cjs</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">   node</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> .icon-preview.cjs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> icon-preview.png</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> heart,bell,settings,yournew</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">        # dark background</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   node .icon-preview.cjs icon-preview-light.png heart,bell,yournew --light   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"># light background</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   rm .icon-preview.cjs</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   ```</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   If you only downloaded </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`SKILL.md`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, save the script next to it as </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`scripts/preview.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> from</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   https://paneui.dev/skills/puffy-icons/scripts/preview.tsx.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   Run it from the project root (it needs the project's </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`react`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`react-dom`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">). PNG output needs</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">   `@resvg/resvg-js`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`npm i -D @resvg/resvg-js`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">); without it the sheet is written as SVG. Any other</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">   way of rendering the components to an image works too, such as a scratch page in your dev server.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">7.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Check the sheet**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> and fix what fails, then render again:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">   -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Padding and optical size match the neighbours (nothing visibly bigger or smaller, nothing</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">     clipped at the edges).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">   -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Weight matches: a puff icon looks as heavy as the other puff icons, a line icon as light as the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">     other line icons.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">   -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Holes and gaps are still open at 14px; no detail turns into a blob.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">   -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Optically centred (a heavy top or a pointed side may need a 0.2–0.4px shift).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">8.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> **Typecheck**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`npx tsc --noEmit`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) and use the icon.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">If you ever re-install or switch the set with shadcn (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`--overwrite`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), it overwrites </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`components/icons.tsx`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">. Keep your</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">own glyphs in a separate section at the end of the file (for example </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">`/* My icons */`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) so they're</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">easy to copy back, or move them to their own file that copies the three primitives above.</span></span></code></pre>26:["$","section",null,{"className":"flex flex-col gap-3","children":[["$","$L1e",null,{"id":"make-your-own-icons","children":"Make your own icons"}],["$","p",null,{"className":"text-pretty text-muted-foreground","children":["Need a glyph the set doesn't have? 34:T78ead,<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light-default github-dark-default" style="--shiki-light:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#e6edf3;--shiki-light-bg:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0d1117" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * paneui icon set. Rounded outline icons on a 24x24 grid (the default style).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Drawing rules: artwork spans about 2-22 (a full circle is r=10); every shape</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * uses `line` (stroke 1.75, round caps and joins, no fill); `dot` only for small</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * filled dots, `solid` only for brand marks; rect rx 4-5 on cards, soft curves</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * everywhere, no sharp corners except arrowheads; currentColor only;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * path/circle/rect/ellipse only, no transforms, masks, clips or &#x3C;g> styling;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * every detail must survive at 16px.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Other styles with the same exports: registry/base-nova/icons/puffy (filled,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * 18x18) and registry/base-nova/icons/outline (generated from puffy).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Usage system (full version in notes/icon-usage.md):</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * - Nav / sidebar / palette icons: 16px, muted; active = foreground; bare.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * - Title / header / card icons sit in an IconChip (@paneui/icon-chip).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * - Inline icons inherit size and colour from their button, badge or text.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * - &#x3C;Name>FilledIcon: the filled glyph for the active item of a nav without a row fill (tab bar).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * - Stroke tier: `weight="bold"` or an ancestor's `icon-bold` class thickens line strokes 1.2x</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *   beside semibold or bold text.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { CSSProperties, SVGProps } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "react"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> IconProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">SVGProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">SVGSVGElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * Stroke tier. `bold` draws line strokes 1.2x thicker so the glyph matches semibold or bold text</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   * beside it. Unset, the icon inherits the tier from an ancestor's `icon-bold` class.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">   */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  weight</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "regular"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "bold"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">};</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> WEIGHT</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { regular: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, bold: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1.2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Line width for the whole glyph, scaled by the weight tier (--icon-stroke-scale). The attribute is the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// fallback for renderers without CSS.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> stroke</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">w</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ({ strokeWidth: w, style: { strokeWidth: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`calc(${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">w</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}px * var(--icon-stroke-scale, 1))`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } });</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 18</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">weight</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">style</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> IconProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> tier</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> stroke</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1.75</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">svg</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      width</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      height</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      viewBox</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"0 0 24 24"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      fill</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"none"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      aria-hidden</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      data-weight</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">weight</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      strokeWidth</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">tier.strokeWidth</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      style</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">tier.style, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(weight </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"--icon-stroke-scale"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">WEIGHT</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[weight] } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> CSSProperties</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">style }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      {...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    ></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">svg</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Round outline stroke: every shape in this style. Its width comes from the &#x3C;svg> (see `stroke`).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> line</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { fill: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"none"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, stroke: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"currentColor"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, strokeLinecap: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"round"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, strokeLinejoin: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"round"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Filled silhouette with the same edge as `line` (filled nav glyphs for the active state).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> fillShape</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { fill: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"currentColor"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, stroke: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"currentColor"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, strokeLinecap: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"round"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, strokeLinejoin: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"round"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, fillRule: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"evenodd"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Small filled dots (info dot, ellipsis, bullets).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> dot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { fill: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"currentColor"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, stroke: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"none"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">// Filled silhouettes, brand marks only.</span></span>
